# ROUND `#524` — `armbdt`, ARM block data transfer

## The defect

`arm_pop()` (LDM) and `arm_push()` (STM) in `cpu_arm_instr.c` each hold one running
`uint32_t addr = *np` and branch on host page residency per register:

* **FAST path** (page resident): `p32[(addr & 0xfff) >> 2]` — the `>> 2` truncates the low two
  bits, which is **architecturally correct**: DDI0100I LDM(1) p.A4-37 and STM(1) p.A4-190 both say
  to *ignore the least significant two bits of the address*.
* **SLOW path** (cold page): passed the **RAW, UNMASKED `addr`** to `memory_rw`, whose
  `offset = paddr & offset_mask` is **page-granularity, not word-rounded**, and whose transfer is a
  raw `memcpy`.

**So an unaligned base spliced bytes across an architectural word boundary, and the same instruction
at the same base returned DIFFERENT BYTES according to host page-cache warmth — a condition
invisible to the guest architecture.**

## The fix (both sites)

```c
-   if (!cpu->memory_rw(cpu, cpu->mem, addr, data,
+   if (!cpu->memory_rw(cpu, cpu->mem, addr & 0xfffffffc, data,
        sizeof(data), MEM_READ, CACHE_DATA)) {      /* and MEM_WRITE at the other site */
```

**The mask must NOT move to function entry:** `if (w_bit) *np = addr;` uses the unmasked running
value, and Mode 4 writeback derives from unmasked Rn. In-tree precedent: the code generator emits
`addr &= 0xffc;` — masking the ACCESS while leaving writeback alone.

## Measured (rung 3, cold debugger, unmodified in-tree `testarm`, pty-driven)

| leg | score |
|---|---|
| fix applied | **25/25** |
| committed HEAD, pre-fix | **17/25** |
| mask at ENTRY instead | **20/25 — killed by the writeback rows** |
| one site only (either) | 21/25 |
| null medium | 6/25 |
| restored baseline | 17/25 = HEAD |

**The pre-existing `arm_writeback_probe.py` scores the entry-mask mutant 22/22 — identical to a
correct build.** The probe is structurally blind to it.

## What the first adversarial pass found (NOT RATIFIED)

1. **The masks are locally correct**, and for an aligned base both changes are strict no-ops
   including across 32-bit wraparound.
2. **"Only two sites on the BDT path" was WRONG.** Two *combined-instruction handlers* replace
   LDM/STM outright and never reach the fixed helpers: `netbsd_memset` does
   `memset(page + (addr & 0xfff), 0, 128)` — raw page offset, no rounding, and its guards check page
   bounds and source values but **never reject an unaligned address**; `netbsd_memcpy` copies
   through raw `& 0xfff` offsets **while its register-publication reads use `& 0xffc`** — one
   handler, two address rules. *(Pre-existing; filed separately, not fixed in this round.)*
3. **Thumb callers entering these helpers get the fix automatically** (no Thumb-specific
   raw-address branch), but a **separate** unfixed issue exists: the helper's PC handling clears
   both low PC bits while Thumb POP's spec preserves the halfword address and specifies
   interworking.
4. **The helpers are not restricted to RAM** — an unaligned address can reach device dispatch, and
   rounding can change a device-relative offset near a device boundary.
5. **A2-42 specifies additional UNPREDICTABLE behaviour for ARMv6 unaligned multiword accesses to
   Device or Strongly Ordered memory**, so "all callers simply ignore those bits" is too broad.
6. The edit rounds the **virtual** address before translation, and `memory_rw` splits translated
   accesses at 1 KB — **"the old call can touch, and fault on, a page the corrected call never
   accesses."**

---

# ROUND `#525` — `wdcaltdigin`, the WDC alt-status handler

## The defect

`DEVICE_ACCESS(wdc_altstatus)` computes `odata = status_byte(d, cpu)` **unconditionally**, then:

```c
	if (writeflag==MEM_READ)
		debug("[ wdc: read from ALTSTATUS ... ]", (int)odata);
	else if (relative_addr != 0) { /* #494: decline the WRITE */ }
	else { /* Alt Ctrl write, #481 */ }
	...
	if (writeflag == MEM_READ)
		data[0] = odata;
	return 1;
```

**`relative_addr` was tested in exactly ONE place — inside the `else` of `writeflag == MEM_READ`.
So the READ path never decoded its offset, and a read of offset 1 (the Drive Address register,
`0x3f7` on the PC layout) returned the ALT-STATUS byte.** The region is registered two bytes wide.

**This is the symmetric cell of `#494`, which closed the WRITE half of the same `if` and left this
one open.** {READ, WRITE} × {offset 0, offset 1} — three cells were closed.

## The fix

```c
	if (writeflag==MEM_READ) {
		if (relative_addr != 0) {
			odata = 0;          /* declined; the common tail writes it out */
		} else
			debug("[ wdc: read from ALTSTATUS ... ]", (int)odata);
	} else if (relative_addr != 0) {
```

Registration deliberately NOT narrowed (owner ruling: decode the read).

## Measured (rung 3, unmodified in-tree machine)

HEAD **14/16** (both offset-1 read rows return the status byte — `0x51` at power-on, `0x58` armed);
fix **16/16**. The status byte S is **nonzero**, which is what makes the clause-(d) mutant
non-vacuous: that mutant keeps the offset guard and deletes `odata = 0`, so if S were 0 the fixed
and broken builds would return the identical byte.

## What the first adversarial pass found (NOT RATIFIED)

1. All four cells verified; the unbraced `else` binds to the inner `if`; braces 6/6.
2. **`status_byte()` has no device-state mutation**, and the `INTERRUPT_DEASSERT` / `int_assert = 0`
   acknowledgement lives in the *main* register handler and is **absent** from this one — so a
   declined read acknowledges nothing. *(Its complete call chain was not verified.)*
3. **The comment overclaimed twice** and both were narrowed: "matches no hardware on any layout"
   (the evidence does not support a universal claim), and "a correct guest reads here expecting a
   floppy effect" (ditto).
4. A refinement: **`DEV_FDC_LENGTH` is 6, so the floppy controller does NOT register `0x3f7`
   today** — the documented collision is **prospective**, not live.
5. **Detector coverage and a selector audit remain unverified.**

# THE QUESTIONS — answer as many as you can judge

1. **Is either fix WRONG?** Not incomplete — wrong. Say so plainly with the mechanism.
2. **`#524`: is `addr & 0xfffffffc` the right form**, and is masking the VIRTUAL address before
   translation the right place, given finding 6 (it can change which page is touched)? **Would
   masking after translation be more correct, or worse?**
3. **`#524`: findings 4 and 5 say the low bits are NOT universally ignorable** — device dispatch and
   ARMv6 Device memory. **Does that make the fix wrong for some caller, or is it a disclosure?**
4. **`#525`: is returning 0 right?** Alternatives: leave the buffer untouched, return `0xff`, return
   the last written value, or fault. **Each has a different failure mode — rank them.**
5. *** THE ONE I MOST WANT ATTACKED, on both rounds: WHAT SELECTOR COULD EITHER REPAIR'S EFFECT BE
   CONDITIONED ON THAT THE DETECTOR NEVER VARIES? *** A sibling round in this project found **six
   mutants scoring a TOTAL ESCAPE** because every detector row probed a single position, and each
   mutant conditioned the repair on a tile or coordinate selector. **`#524` has 25 rows and `#525`
   has 16. Name a conditioning that both a plausible edit could produce and the rows could not
   see.**
6. **Should `#524` and `#525` be one commit or two?** They are different files, different devices,
   different defects — but both are "a handler ignored part of its own address."
7. **What is the smallest edit that breaks either fix and still passes its detector?**

**An honest "I could not settle that from this packet" is a real answer and is preferred over a
guess.** If you can read files and your line numbers disagree with this packet by hundreds, you are
reading a different copy of the tree — say so.

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
agy_armbdt_witness_20260911.py -- Rung-3 witness for ARM block data transfer (armbdt).

Tests all four callers of arm_pop / arm_push:
  1. X(bdt_load)          (ARM LDM)
  2. X(bdt_store)         (ARM STM)
  3. Thumb POP            (cpu_arm.c:1677)
  4. Thumb PUSH           (cpu_arm.c:1670)

Under cold debugger on unmodified in-tree machine testarm.
Core row shape: identical instruction against identical unaligned base executed
COLD and WARM, asserting both produce identical architecturally-correct bytes.
Discriminates writeback (W=1) unmasked arithmetic result to kill mask-at-entry mutant.
Includes liveness control and attribution control asserting specific byte splice.
"""

import os, sys, pty, select, time, re

BIN = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "/tmp/gxarmbdt_clone/gxemul"
LEG = sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv) > 2 else "BASE"
STUB = "/tmp/stub_armbdt.bin"
if not os.path.exists(STUB):
    with open(STUB, "wb") as f:
        f.write((0xE1A00000).to_bytes(4, "little"))

CODE = 0x00008000
PAGE_LDM = 0x00020000
PAGE_STM = 0x00021000
PAGE_TPOP = 0x00022000
PAGE_TPUSH = 0x00023000

class GXemulSession:
    def __init__(self, bin_path, timeout=30):
        self.bin_path = bin_path
        self.pid, self.fd = pty.fork()
        if self.pid == 0:
            os.execvp(bin_path, [bin_path, "-V", "-A", "-T", "-E", "testarm", "-M", "64", "0x%x:%s" % (CODE, STUB)])
            os._exit(127)
        self.buf = ""
        self.dead = False
        self.wait_prompt(timeout)

    def rd(self, t=0.2):
        r, _, _ = select.select([self.fd], [], [], t)
        if self.fd not in r:
            return False
        try:
            d = os.read(self.fd, 65536)
        except OSError:
            self.dead = True
            return False
        if not d:
            self.dead = True
            return False
        self.buf += d.decode("latin1", "replace")
        return True

    def wait_prompt(self, timeout=20):
        t0 = time.time()
        while time.time() - t0 < timeout:
            if self.dead:
                return False
            self.rd(0.1)
            if self.buf.rstrip().endswith("GXemul>"):
                return True
        return False

    def send(self, cmd_str, timeout=15):
        if self.dead:
            return ""
        mark = len(self.buf)
        b = (cmd_str + "\n").encode("latin1")
        try:
            os.write(self.fd, b)
        except OSError:
            self.dead = True
            return ""
        t0 = time.time()
        while time.time() - t0 < timeout:
            if self.dead:
                return ""
            self.rd(0.1)
            if len(self.buf) > mark and self.buf[mark:].rstrip().endswith("GXemul>"):
                return self.buf[mark:]
        return ""

    def run_prog(self, prog_words, timeout=3.0):
        for i, w in enumerate(prog_words):
            self.send("put w 0x%x, 0x%08x" % (CODE + 4*i, w))
        self.send("pc=0x%x" % CODE)
        mark = len(self.buf)
        try:
            os.write(self.fd, b"continue\n")
        except OSError:
            self.dead = True
            return False
        t0 = time.time()
        while time.time() - t0 < timeout:
            self.rd(0.1)
        try:
            os.write(self.fd, b"\x03")
        except OSError:
            pass
        return self.wait_prompt(timeout=5)

    def get_reg(self, rname):
        raw = self.send("print %s" % rname)
        m = re.search(r"0x([0-9a-fA-F]+)", raw)
        return int(m.group(1), 16) if m else None

    def read_mem32(self, addr):
        raw = self.send("dump 0x%x 0x%x" % (addr, addr + 8))
        hex_addr = "0x%08x" % (addr & ~0xF)
        offset_in_16 = addr & 0xF
        for line in raw.splitlines():
            if line.startswith(hex_addr):
                parts = line[12:48].split()
                idx = offset_in_16 // 4
                if idx < len(parts):
                    hex_str = parts[idx]
                    if len(hex_str) == 8:
                        b0 = int(hex_str[0:2], 16)
                        b1 = int(hex_str[2:4], 16)
                        b2 = int(hex_str[4:6], 16)
                        b3 = int(hex_str[6:8], 16)
                        return b0 | (b1 << 8) | (b2 << 16) | (b3 << 24)
        return None

    def get_tlb_page(self, vaddr):
        raw = self.send("tlbdump 0,r")
        hex_addr = "0x%08x" % (vaddr & ~0xFFF)
        for line in raw.splitlines():
            if hex_addr in line:
                m_load = re.search(r"load=([yn])", line)
                m_store = re.search(r"store=([yn])", line)
                load_ok = (m_load.group(1) == "y") if m_load else False
                store_ok = (m_store.group(1) == "y") if m_store else False
                return True, load_ok, store_ok
        return False, False, False

    def close(self):
        try:
            self.send("quit", timeout=2)
            os.kill(self.pid, 9)
            os.waitpid(self.pid, 0)
        except Exception:
            pass

def main():
    print(f"=== ARM BLOCK DATA TRANSFER WITNESS (armbdt)  LEG={LEG} ===")
    print(f"Binary: {BIN}")
    print("Core symptom: Unaligned base splices bytes across word boundary on cold host pages")
    print("Mandatory writeback: Unmasked Rn arithmetic preserved under W=1 Mode 4 transfers")
    print("")

    results = []
    failing = []

    def record(name, kind, ok, detail=""):
        res_str = "ok" if ok else "FAIL"
        print(f"{name:<34}  {kind:<8}  {detail:<38}  {res_str}", flush=True)
        results.append((name, kind, ok, detail))
        if not ok:
            failing.append(name)

    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    # 1. LIVENESS CONTROL: Aligned LDM/STM round-trip at 0x10000
    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    s = GXemulSession(BIN)
    s.send("put w 0x10000, 0xCAFEBABE")
    prog_ctrl = [0xe3a00801, 0xe8900002, 0xeafffffe]
    s.run_prog(prog_ctrl)
    val = s.get_reg("r1")
    s.close()
    ctrl_live = (val == 0xCAFEBABE)
    record("CONTROL_LIVENESS", "CTRL", ctrl_live, f"r1={val:#010x} want 0xcafebabe")

    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    # 2. CALLER 1: ARM LDM (X(bdt_load)) - COLD vs WARM
    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    # Cold run: seed 0x20000 with put b (CACHE_NONE | NO_EXCEPTIONS)
    s = GXemulSession(BIN)
    bytes_ldm = [0x44, 0x33, 0x22, 0x11, 0x88, 0x77, 0x66, 0x55]
    for i, b in enumerate(bytes_ldm):
        s.send("put b 0x%x, 0x%02x" % (PAGE_LDM + i, b))
    in_tlb_cold, _, _ = s.get_tlb_page(PAGE_LDM)

    prog_ldm = [0xe3a00802, 0xe2800001, 0xe8900002, 0xeafffffe]
    s.run_prog(prog_ldm)
    r1_cold = s.get_reg("r1")
    s.close()

    # Warm run: seed 0x20000 with put w (CACHE_DATA warms page)
    s = GXemulSession(BIN)
    s.send("put w 0x%x, 0x11223344" % PAGE_LDM)
    s.send("put w 0x%x, 0x55667788" % (PAGE_LDM + 4))
    in_tlb_warm, _, _ = s.get_tlb_page(PAGE_LDM)
    s.run_prog(prog_ldm)
    r1_warm = s.get_reg("r1")
    s.close()

    # Attribution control (pre-fix bug produces exact spliced bytes)
    record("CONTROL_ATTRIB_PLUS1", "CTRL-A", (r1_cold == 0x88112233 or r1_cold == 0x11223344),
           f"cold r1={r1_cold:#010x} (bug=0x88112233)")

    record("ARM_LDM_PATH_COLD", "PATH", (not in_tlb_cold),
           f"page resident before cold={in_tlb_cold} want False")
    record("ARM_LDM_PATH_WARM", "PATH", in_tlb_warm,
           f"page resident before warm={in_tlb_warm} want True")
    record("ARM_LDM_COLD_DATA", "DATA", (r1_cold == 0x11223344),
           f"r1={r1_cold:#010x} want 0x11223344 (bug 0x88112233)")
    record("ARM_LDM_WARM_DATA", "DATA", (r1_warm == 0x11223344),
           f"r1={r1_warm:#010x} want 0x11223344")
    record("ARM_LDM_DIV_CHECK", "DISC", (r1_cold == r1_warm and r1_cold == 0x11223344),
           f"cold={r1_cold:#010x} warm={r1_warm:#010x}")

    # Writeback 1-reg: ldmia r0!, {r1} with unaligned base 0x20001
    s = GXemulSession(BIN)
    for i, b in enumerate(bytes_ldm):
        s.send("put b 0x%x, 0x%02x" % (PAGE_LDM + i, b))
    prog_ldm_wb1 = [0xe3a00802, 0xe2800001, 0xe8b00002, 0xeafffffe]
    s.run_prog(prog_ldm_wb1)
    r0_wb1 = s.get_reg("r0")
    s.close()
    record("ARM_LDM_WB_UNAL1", "WB", (r0_wb1 == 0x20005),
           f"r0={r0_wb1:#010x} want 0x00020005 (mutant 0x00020004)")

    # Writeback 2-regs: ldmia r0!, {r1, r2} with unaligned base 0x20001
    s = GXemulSession(BIN)
    for i, b in enumerate(bytes_ldm):
        s.send("put b 0x%x, 0x%02x" % (PAGE_LDM + i, b))
    prog_ldm_wb2 = [0xe3a00802, 0xe2800001, 0xe8b00006, 0xeafffffe]
    s.run_prog(prog_ldm_wb2)
    r0_wb2 = s.get_reg("r0")
    s.close()
    record("ARM_LDM_WB_UNAL2", "WB", (r0_wb2 == 0x20009),
           f"r0={r0_wb2:#010x} want 0x00020009 (mutant 0x00020008)")

    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    # 3. CALLER 2: ARM STM (X(bdt_store)) - COLD vs WARM
    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    # Cold STM: seed 0x21000..0x21007 with zeroes via put b
    s = GXemulSession(BIN)
    for i in range(8):
        s.send("put b 0x%x, 0x00" % (PAGE_STM + i))
    in_tlb_stm_cold, _, _ = s.get_tlb_page(PAGE_STM)

    prog_stm = [
        0xe59f0008, 0xe59f1008, 0xe8800002, 0xeafffffe,
        0x00021001, 0x11223344
    ]
    s.run_prog(prog_stm)
    r4_stm_cold = s.read_mem32(PAGE_STM)
    r5_stm_cold = s.read_mem32(PAGE_STM + 4)
    s.close()

    # Warm STM: seed 0x21000..0x21007 with zeroes via put w
    s = GXemulSession(BIN)
    s.send("put w 0x%x, 0x00000000" % PAGE_STM)
    s.send("put w 0x%x, 0x00000000" % (PAGE_STM + 4))
    in_tlb_stm_warm, _, _ = s.get_tlb_page(PAGE_STM)
    s.run_prog(prog_stm)
    r4_stm_warm = s.read_mem32(PAGE_STM)
    r5_stm_warm = s.read_mem32(PAGE_STM + 4)
    s.close()

    record("ARM_STM_PATH_COLD", "PATH", (not in_tlb_stm_cold),
           f"page resident before cold={in_tlb_stm_cold} want False")
    record("ARM_STM_PATH_WARM", "PATH", in_tlb_stm_warm,
           f"page resident before warm={in_tlb_stm_warm} want True")
    record("ARM_STM_COLD_DATA", "DATA", (r4_stm_cold == 0x11223344),
           f"w0={r4_stm_cold:#010x} want 0x11223344 (bug 0x22334400)")
    record("ARM_STM_COLD_CLOBBER", "DATA", (r5_stm_cold == 0x00000000),
           f"w1={r5_stm_cold:#010x} want 0x00000000 (bug 0x00000011)")
    record("ARM_STM_WARM_DATA", "DATA", (r4_stm_warm == 0x11223344 and r5_stm_warm == 0),
           f"w0={r4_stm_warm:#010x} w1={r5_stm_warm:#010x}")
    record("ARM_STM_DIV_CHECK", "DISC", (r4_stm_cold == r4_stm_warm and r4_stm_cold == 0x11223344),
           f"cold={r4_stm_cold:#010x} warm={r4_stm_warm:#010x}")

    # Writeback STM: stmia r0!, {r1} with unaligned base 0x21001
    s = GXemulSession(BIN)
    for i in range(8):
        s.send("put b 0x%x, 0x00" % (PAGE_STM + i))
    prog_stm_wb = [
        0xe59f0008, 0xe59f1008, 0xe8a00002, 0xeafffffe,
        0x00021001, 0x11223344
    ]
    s.run_prog(prog_stm_wb)
    r0_stm_wb = s.get_reg("r0")
    s.close()
    record("ARM_STM_WB_UNAL", "WB", (r0_stm_wb == 0x21005),
           f"r0={r0_stm_wb:#010x} want 0x00021005 (mutant 0x00021004)")

    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    # 4. CALLER 3: Thumb POP (cpu_arm.c:1677) - COLD vs WARM
    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    # Cold Thumb POP:
    s = GXemulSession(BIN)
    for i, b in enumerate(bytes_ldm):
        s.send("put b 0x%x, 0x%02x" % (PAGE_TPOP + i, b))
    prog_tpop = [0xe59f0008, 0xe1a0d000, 0xe28f0005, 0xe12fff10, 0x00022001, 0xe7febc02]
    s.run_prog(prog_tpop)
    r1_tpop_cold = s.get_reg("r1")
    sp_tpop_cold = s.get_reg("sp")
    s.close()

    # Warm Thumb POP:
    s = GXemulSession(BIN)
    s.send("put w 0x%x, 0x11223344" % PAGE_TPOP)
    s.send("put w 0x%x, 0x55667788" % (PAGE_TPOP + 4))
    s.run_prog(prog_tpop)
    r1_tpop_warm = s.get_reg("r1")
    s.close()

    record("THUMB_POP_COLD_DATA", "DATA", (r1_tpop_cold == 0x11223344),
           f"r1={r1_tpop_cold:#010x} want 0x11223344 (bug 0x88112233)")
    record("THUMB_POP_WARM_DATA", "DATA", (r1_tpop_warm == 0x11223344),
           f"r1={r1_tpop_warm:#010x} want 0x11223344")
    record("THUMB_POP_DIV_CHECK", "DISC", (r1_tpop_cold == r1_tpop_warm and r1_tpop_cold == 0x11223344),
           f"cold={r1_tpop_cold:#010x} warm={r1_tpop_warm:#010x}")
    record("THUMB_POP_WB_UNAL", "WB", (sp_tpop_cold == 0x22005),
           f"sp={sp_tpop_cold:#010x} want 0x00022005 (mutant 0x00022004)")

    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    # 5. CALLER 4: Thumb PUSH (cpu_arm.c:1670) - COLD vs WARM
    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    # Thumb push {r1} decrements sp by 4 before store (stmdb sp!, {r1}).
    # Initial sp = 0x23005 -> decrements to 0x23001, stores r1 = 0x11223344.
    # Cold Thumb PUSH:
    s = GXemulSession(BIN)
    for i in range(8):
        s.send("put b 0x%x, 0x00" % (PAGE_TPUSH + i))
    prog_tpush = [
        0xe59f0014,  # 0x8000: ldr r0, [pc, #20] -> loads 0x23005 at 0x801c
        0xe1a0d000,  # 0x8004: mov sp, r0
        0xe59f1010,  # 0x8008: ldr r1, [pc, #16] -> loads 0x11223344 at 0x8020
        0xe28f0005,  # 0x800c: add r0, pc, #5    -> 0x8019 (target 0x8018 | 1)
        0xe12fff10,  # 0x8010: bx r0
        0xe1a00000,  # 0x8014: nop
        0xe7feb402,  # 0x8018: push {r1} (0xb402), b . (0xe7fe)
        0x00023005,  # 0x801c
        0x11223344   # 0x8020
    ]
    s.run_prog(prog_tpush)
    sp_tpush_cold = s.get_reg("sp")
    r4_tpush_cold = s.read_mem32(PAGE_TPUSH)
    r5_tpush_cold = s.read_mem32(PAGE_TPUSH + 4)
    s.close()

    # Warm Thumb PUSH:
    s = GXemulSession(BIN)
    s.send("put w 0x%x, 0x00000000" % PAGE_TPUSH)
    s.send("put w 0x%x, 0x00000000" % (PAGE_TPUSH + 4))
    s.run_prog(prog_tpush)
    r4_tpush_warm = s.read_mem32(PAGE_TPUSH)
    r5_tpush_warm = s.read_mem32(PAGE_TPUSH + 4)
    s.close()

    record("THUMB_PUSH_COLD_DATA", "DATA", (r4_tpush_cold == 0x11223344),
           f"w0={r4_tpush_cold:#010x} want 0x11223344 (bug 0x22334400)")
    record("THUMB_PUSH_COLD_CLOBBER", "DATA", (r5_tpush_cold == 0x00000000),
           f"w1={r5_tpush_cold:#010x} want 0x00000000 (bug 0x00000011)")
    record("THUMB_PUSH_WARM_DATA", "DATA", (r4_tpush_warm == 0x11223344 and r5_tpush_warm == 0),
           f"w0={r4_tpush_warm:#010x} w1={r5_tpush_warm:#010x}")
    record("THUMB_PUSH_DIV_CHECK", "DISC", (r4_tpush_cold == r4_tpush_warm and r4_tpush_cold == 0x11223344),
           f"cold={r4_tpush_cold:#010x} warm={r4_tpush_warm:#010x}")
    record("THUMB_PUSH_WB_UNAL", "WB", (sp_tpush_cold == 0x23001),
           f"sp={sp_tpush_cold:#010x} want 0x00023001 (mutant 0x00023000)")

    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    # SUMMARY & SENTINEL
    # -------------------------------------------------------------
    n_ok = sum(1 for _, _, ok, _ in results if ok)
    n_total = len(results)
    print("")
    print(f"SCORE {n_ok}/{n_total}")
    if failing:
        print(f"FAILING: {','.join(failing)}")
    else:
        print("FAILING: none")
    print("ARMBDT_WITNESS_DONE")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
"""wdcaltdigin -- RUNG-3 WITNESS AND KILL-TABLE DETECTOR
Unmodified in-tree machine: evbmips -e malta (PIIX4 IDE / wdc).
Access direction: READ from offset 1 (Drive Address register / digin) vs offset 0 (Alt Status).

Defect:
  DEVICE_ACCESS(wdc_altstatus) at src/devices/dev_wdc.c:609 unconditionally calculates
  odata = status_byte(d, cpu) and on MEM_READ assigns data[0] = odata without checking relative_addr.
  Offset 1 (0x3f7) incorrectly returns status byte S rather than declining the unmapped read.
"""
import os
import pty
import re
import select
import sys
import time

BINARY = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "/tmp/gxaltdigin_clone/gxemul"
LEG = sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv) > 2 else "HEAD"
DISK = sys.argv[3] if len(sys.argv) > 3 else "/tmp/gxaltdigin_clone/scratch_disk.img"
OUTDIR = sys.argv[4] if len(sys.argv) > 4 else "/tmp/gxaltdigin_clone/run"

BASE = 0xffffffff80100000          # KSEG0 physical 0x00100000
WDC_HI, WDC_LO = 0xb800, 0x01f0    # wdc register file, KSEG1 physical 0x180001f0
ALT_HI, ALT_LO = 0xb800, 0x03f6    # wdc_altstatus region, KSEG1 physical 0x180003f6
RAM_HI = 0x8020                    # KSEG0 scratch physical 0x00200000

# Register offsets from wdc base
R_DATA, R_SECCNT, R_SECTOR = 0, 2, 3
R_CYL_LO, R_CYL_HI, R_SDH = 4, 5, 6
R_STATUS = 7

# Status bits
DRDY, DSC, DRQ, ERR = 0x40, 0x10, 0x08, 0x01
ST_POWERON = DRDY | DSC | ERR      # 0x51
ST_IDLE = DRDY | DSC               # 0x50
ST_ARMED = DRDY | DSC | DRQ        # 0x58

WDCC_WRITE = 0x30
WDCTL_RST = 0x04
WDCTL_IDS = 0x02
SRST_POKE = WDCTL_IDS | WDCTL_RST  # 0x06
FF = 0xff

STAGE = [0x71, 0x72, 0x73, 0x74, 0x75, 0x76, 0x77, 0x78]

zero, v0, v1, a0, a1, a2, a3 = 0, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7
t0, t1, t2, t3 = 8, 9, 10, 11
s0, s1, s2, s3, s4, s5, s6, s7 = 16, 17, 18, 19, 20, 21, 22, 23
t8, t9 = 24, 25

def I(op, rs, rt, imm):
    return (op << 26) | (rs << 21) | (rt << 16) | (imm & 0xffff)

def lui(rt, imm):
    return I(0x0F, 0, rt, imm)

def ori(rt, rs, imm):
    return I(0x0D, rs, rt, imm)

def addiu(rt, rs, imm):
    return I(0x09, rs, rt, imm)

def sb(rt, off, base):
    return I(0x28, base, rt, off)

def lbu(rt, off, base):
    return I(0x24, base, rt, off)

def beq(rs, rt, off):
    return I(0x04, rs, rt, off)

NOP = 0
NAME = {zero: "zr", v0: "v0", v1: "v1", a0: "a0", a1: "a1", a2: "a2", a3: "a3",
        t0: "t0", t1: "t1", t2: "t2", t3: "t3", s0: "s0", s1: "s1", s2: "s2",
        s3: "s3", s4: "s4", s5: "s5", s6: "s6", s7: "s7", t8: "t8", t9: "t9"}

prog = []

def emit(word, mn):
    prog.append((word, mn))

def e_li(rt, v):
    emit(addiu(rt, zero, v), "addiu %s,zr,%d" % (NAME[rt], v))

def e_sb(rt, off, base):
    emit(sb(rt, off, base), "sb %s,%d(%s)" % (NAME[rt], off, NAME[base]))

def e_lbu(rt, off, base):
    emit(lbu(rt, off, base), "lbu %s,%d(%s)" % (NAME[rt], off, NAME[base]))

def e_store(val, off, base):
    e_li(t3, val)
    e_sb(t3, off, base)

def arm_a_write():
    e_store(0xa0, R_SDH, t0)
    e_store(0x01, R_SECCNT, t0)
    e_store(0x01, R_SECTOR, t0)
    e_store(0x00, R_CYL_LO, t0)
    e_store(0x00, R_CYL_HI, t0)
    e_store(WDCC_WRITE, R_STATUS, t0)
    for b in STAGE:
        e_store(b, R_DATA, t0)

# Instruction emission
# Setup base registers
emit(lui(t0, WDC_HI), "lui t0,0xb800")
emit(ori(t0, t0, WDC_LO), "ori t0,t0,0x01f0")
emit(lui(t1, ALT_HI), "lui t1,0xb800")
emit(ori(t1, t1, ALT_LO), "ori t1,t1,0x03f6")
emit(lui(t2, RAM_HI), "lui t2,0x8020")

# 1. Controls
# RAM liveness
e_store(0x55, 0, t2)
e_lbu(v0, 0, t2)

# Register liveness
e_store(0x55, R_CYL_LO, t0)
e_store(0x66, R_CYL_HI, t0)
e_lbu(v1, R_CYL_LO, t0)
e_lbu(a0, R_CYL_HI, t0)

# FIFO signature
e_store(0x5a, R_DATA, t0)
e_store(0xa5, R_DATA, t0)
e_lbu(a1, R_DATA, t0)
e_lbu(a2, R_DATA, t0)

# Power-on status signature
e_lbu(a3, R_STATUS, t0)

# 2. Phase R1: Altstatus reads at power-on
e_lbu(s0, 0, t1)    # offset 0 (0x3f6) -> ACCEPT: returns S=0x51
e_lbu(s1, 1, t1)    # offset 1 (0x3f7) -> CORE: HEAD returns 0x51, BASE returns 0x00

# 3. Phase R2: Altstatus reads while armed
arm_a_write()
e_lbu(s2, R_STATUS, t0)  # Armed status at +7 -> 0x58 (DRQ set)
e_lbu(s3, 0, t1)         # offset 0 (0x3f6) -> ACCEPT: returns S_armed=0x58
e_lbu(s4, 1, t1)         # offset 1 (0x3f7) -> CORE: HEAD returns 0x58, BASE returns 0x00

# 4. Phase W1: WRITE to offset 0 (#481 soft reset unregressed)
e_store(SRST_POKE, 0, t1)
e_lbu(s5, R_STATUS, t0)  # DRQ cleared -> 0x50
e_lbu(s6, R_DATA, t0)    # Ring purged -> 0xff

# 5. Phase W2: WRITE to offset 1 (#494 decline unregressed)
arm_a_write()
e_lbu(s7, R_STATUS, t0)  # Armed status -> 0x58
e_store(SRST_POKE, 1, t1)
e_lbu(t8, R_STATUS, t0)  # DRQ still set -> 0x58
e_lbu(t9, R_DATA, t0)    # Staged bytes intact -> 0x71

HALT = len(prog)
emit(beq(zero, zero, -1), "b 0x")
emit(NOP, "nop")
HALT_ADDR = BASE + HALT * 4

REGS = ("v0", "v1", "a0", "a1", "a2", "a3",
        "s0", "s1", "s2", "s3", "s4", "s5", "s6", "s7", "t8", "t9")

# Expected values on the BASE (candidate fix) build
ROW_DEFS = [
    ("CONTROL_RAM_LIVENESS",       "v0", 0x55, "control", "RAM byte round-trip"),
    ("CONTROL_CYL_LO_LIVENESS",    "v1", 0x55, "control", "cyl_lo readback"),
    ("CONTROL_CYL_HI_LIVENESS",    "a0", 0x66, "control", "cyl_hi readback"),
    ("CONTROL_FIFO_DEVSIG_1",      "a1", 0x5a, "control", "FIFO data port 1st read"),
    ("CONTROL_FIFO_DEVSIG_2",      "a2", 0xa5, "control", "FIFO data port 2nd read"),
    ("CONTROL_STATUS_POWERON",     "a3", ST_POWERON, "control", "Status byte at power-on (+7)"),
    ("ACCEPT_READ_OFF0_POWERON",   "s0", ST_POWERON, "accept",  "Legal offset-0 read returns S (0x51 != 0)"),
    ("CORE_READ_OFF1_POWERON",     "s1", 0x00,       "core",    "Offset-1 read declined: want 0x00 != S"),
    ("CONTROL_STATUS_ARMED",       "s2", ST_ARMED,   "control", "Armed status (+7) has DRQ set (0x58)"),
    ("ACCEPT_READ_OFF0_ARMED",     "s3", ST_ARMED,   "accept",  "Legal offset-0 read returns S_armed (0x58 != 0)"),
    ("CORE_READ_OFF1_ARMED",       "s4", 0x00,       "core",    "Offset-1 read armed declined: want 0x00 != S_armed"),
    ("WRITE0_SRST_DRQ_CLEAR",      "s5", ST_IDLE,    "accept",  "#481: offset-0 SRST clears DRQ (0x50)"),
    ("WRITE0_SRST_RING_PURGED",    "s6", FF,         "accept",  "#481: offset-0 SRST purges ring (0xff)"),
    ("CONTROL_STATUS_ARMED_W2",    "s7", ST_ARMED,   "control", "Re-armed status for Phase W2 (0x58)"),
    ("WRITE1_DECLINE_DRQ_SET",     "t8", ST_ARMED,   "accept",  "#494: offset-1 SRST declined: DRQ still set (0x58)"),
    ("WRITE1_DECLINE_RING_INTACT", "t9", 0x71,       "accept",  "#494: offset-1 SRST declined: byte 0x71 intact"),
]

class Emu(object):
    def __init__(self, argv, boot_wait=5.0):
        self.buf = bytearray()
        self.pid, self.fd = pty.fork()
        if self.pid == 0:
            try:
                os.execvp(argv[0], argv)
            finally:
                os._exit(127)
        self.drain(boot_wait)

    def drain(self, seconds):
        end = time.time() + seconds
        while time.time() < end:
            r, _, _ = select.select([self.fd], [], [], 0.05)
            if self.fd in r:
                try:
                    d = os.read(self.fd, 65536)
                except OSError:
                    return
                if not d:
                    return
                self.buf.extend(d)

    def send(self, cmd, wait=0.4):
        start = len(self.buf)
        data = (cmd + "\n").encode()
        view = memoryview(data)
        while len(view):
            n = os.write(self.fd, view)
            view = view[n:]
            if len(view):
                self.drain(0.05)
        self.drain(wait)
        return bytes(self.buf[start:]).decode("latin1", "replace")

    def close(self, log):
        try:
            os.write(self.fd, b"quit\n")
            self.drain(0.4)
        except OSError:
            pass
        for fn in (lambda: os.close(self.fd),
                   lambda: os.kill(self.pid, 9),
                   lambda: os.waitpid(self.pid, 0)):
            try:
                fn()
            except OSError:
                pass
        with open(log, "w") as f:
            f.write(bytes(self.buf).decode("latin1", "replace"))

def reg(emu, name):
    for _ in range(5):
        out = emu.send("print %s" % name, wait=0.35)
        m = re.findall(r"0x[0-9a-fA-F]+", out)
        if m:
            return int(m[-1], 16)
        emu.drain(0.25)
    return None

def main():
    os.makedirs(OUTDIR, exist_ok=True)
    if not os.path.isfile(DISK):
        with open(DISK, "wb") as f:
            f.write(b"\0" * (8 * 1024 * 1024))

    rawbin = os.path.join(OUTDIR, "altdigin_%s.bin" % LEG)
    with open(rawbin, "wb") as f:
        for w, _ in prog:
            f.write(bytes([w & 0xff, (w >> 8) & 0xff, (w >> 16) & 0xff, (w >> 24) & 0xff]))

    argv = [BINARY, "-V", "-E", "evbmips", "-e", "malta",
            "-d", "iR:" + DISK, "0x%x:%s" % (BASE, rawbin)]

    print("==============================================================================")
    print("wdcaltdigin Rung-3 Witness -- Leg: %s" % LEG)
    print("==============================================================================")
    print("date       %s" % time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"))
    print("binary     %s" % BINARY)
    print("command    %s" % " ".join(argv))
    sys.stdout.flush()

    emu = Emu(argv)

    # Control 0 check
    dis = emu.send("unassemble 0x%x 0x%x" % (BASE, BASE + len(prog) * 4), wait=3.0)
    seen = {}
    for line in dis.splitlines():
        m = re.match(r"^\s*([0-9a-f]{8,16}):\s+([0-9a-f]{8})\s+(?:<-)?\s*(.*)$", line)
        if m:
            seen[int(m.group(1), 16) & 0xffffffff] = (m.group(2), m.group(3).strip())

    w_ok = 0
    for i, (w, _) in enumerate(prog):
        addr = (BASE + i * 4) & 0xffffffff
        hit = seen.get(addr)
        if hit and hit[0] == "%08x" % w:
            w_ok += 1
    print("Control 0 disassembly: %d of %d words verified match" % (w_ok, len(prog)))

    emu.send("breakpoint add 0x%x" % HALT_ADDR, wait=0.5)
    t = time.time()
    emu.send("continue", wait=1.0)
    deadline = time.time() + 60
    hit = False
    while time.time() < deadline:
        emu.drain(0.5)
        tail = bytes(emu.buf[-4000:]).decode("latin1", "replace")
        if "BREAKPOINT" in tail.upper():
            hit = True
            break
    elapsed = time.time() - t
    print("Breakpoint at 0x%x reached: %s (%.2f s)" % (HALT_ADDR, hit, elapsed))

    got = {}
    for nm in REGS:
        v = reg(emu, nm)
        got[nm] = None if v is None else (v & 0xff)

    logpath = os.path.join(OUTDIR, "transcript_%s.log" % LEG)
    emu.close(logpath)

    # Report measured S explicitly
    s_pwr = got.get("s0")
    s_arm = got.get("s3")
    off1_pwr = got.get("s1")
    off1_arm = got.get("s4")
    print("------------------------------------------------------------------------------")
    print("MEASURED STATUS & OFFSET-1 SAMPLES:")
    print("  Power-on Altstatus (off 0): S = 0x%02x (want 0x51)" % (s_pwr if s_pwr is not None else 0))
    print("  Power-on Offset 1 (off 1):  0x%02x (BASE want 0x00, HEAD defect is 0x%02x)" %
          (off1_pwr if off1_pwr is not None else 0, s_pwr if s_pwr is not None else 0))
    print("  Armed Altstatus (off 0):    S_armed = 0x%02x (want 0x58)" % (s_arm if s_arm is not None else 0))
    print("  Armed Offset 1 (off 1):     0x%02x (BASE want 0x00, HEAD defect is 0x%02x)" %
          (off1_arm if off1_arm is not None else 0, s_arm if s_arm is not None else 0))
    print("------------------------------------------------------------------------------")

    # Evaluate rows
    npass = 0
    fails = []
    print("%-28s %-4s %-8s %-8s %-6s %s" % ("ROW NAME", "REG", "WANT", "GOT", "STATUS", "DESCRIPTION"))
    print("-" * 80)
    for name, rname, want, kind, desc in ROW_DEFS:
        g = got.get(rname)
        ok = (g == want)
        if ok:
            npass += 1
        else:
            fails.append((name, rname, want, g))
        print("%-28s %-4s 0x%02x     0x%02x     %-6s %s" %
              (name, rname, want, g if g is not None else 0, "PASS" if ok else "FAIL", desc))

    print("-" * 80)
    print("SCORE %d/%d" % (npass, len(ROW_DEFS)))
    if fails:
        print("FAILING ROWS (%d):" % len(fails))
        for name, rname, want, g in fails:
            print("  FAIL: %s (%s) want 0x%02x got 0x%02x" % (name, rname, want, g if g is not None else 0))
    else:
        print("ALL ROWS PASSED CLEANLY.")

    print("WDCALTDIGIN_WITNESS_DONE")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

#include "cpu_arm_instr_misc.c"


/*
 *  Shared between regular ARM and the THUMB encoded 'pop'.
 */
void arm_pop(struct cpu* cpu, uint32_t* np, int p_bit, int u_bit, int s_bit, int w_bit, uint32_t iw)
{
	uint32_t addr = *np;
	unsigned char data[4];
	unsigned char *page;
	int i, return_flag = 0;
	uint32_t new_values[16];

	if (s_bit) {
		/*  Load to USR registers:  */
		if ((cpu->cd.arm.cpsr & ARM_FLAG_MODE) == ARM_MODE_USR32) {
			fatal("[ bdt_load: s-bit: in usermode? ]\n");
			s_bit = 0;
		}
		if (iw & 0x8000) {
			s_bit = 0;
			return_flag = 1;
		}
	}

	for (i=(u_bit? 0 : 15); i>=0 && i<=15; i+=(u_bit? 1 : -1)) {
		uint32_t value;

		if (!((iw >> i) & 1)) {
			/*  Skip register i:  */
			continue;
		}

		if (p_bit) {
			if (u_bit)
				addr += sizeof(uint32_t);
			else
				addr -= sizeof(uint32_t);
		}

		page = cpu->cd.arm.host_load[addr >> 12];
		if (page != NULL) {
			uint32_t *p32 = (uint32_t *) page;
			value = p32[(addr & 0xfff) >> 2];
			/*  Change byte order of value if
			    host and emulated endianness differ:  */
#ifdef HOST_LITTLE_ENDIAN
			if (cpu->byte_order == EMUL_BIG_ENDIAN)
#else
			if (cpu->byte_order == EMUL_LITTLE_ENDIAN)
#endif
				value = ((value & 0xff) << 24) |
				    ((value & 0xff00) << 8) |
				    ((value & 0xff0000) >> 8) |
				    ((value & 0xff000000) >> 24);
		} else {
			/*  #524 (armbdt): MASK THE ACCESS, NEVER THE RUNNING
			    `addr'.  DDI0100I LDM(1) p.A4-37 says to ignore the
			    low two bits of the address; the FAST path above gets
			    that free from `(addr & 0xfff) >> 2', and this slow
			    path passed the RAW addr to memory_rw, whose
			    `offset = paddr & offset_mask' is PAGE-granularity and
			    whose transfer is a raw memcpy -- so an unaligned base
			    SPLICED BYTES ACROSS A WORD BOUNDARY and the same
			    instruction returned different bytes according to host
			    page-cache warmth, a condition invisible to the guest.

			    The mask must NOT move to the function entry: `*np =
			    addr' at the writeback below uses the unmasked running
			    value, and Mode 4 writeback derives from unmasked Rn.
			    Measured: an entry-mask mutant scores 20/25 on this
			    round's detector, killed by the writeback rows alone --
			    while the pre-existing arm_writeback_probe.py scores it
			    22/22, identical to a correct build.  The generated
			    fast dispatch already separates the two the same way
			    (generate_arm_multi.c, `addr &= 0xffc').  */
			if (!cpu->memory_rw(cpu, cpu->mem, addr & 0xfffffffc, data,
			    sizeof(data), MEM_READ, CACHE_DATA)) {
				/*  load failed  */
				return;
			}
			if (cpu->byte_order == EMUL_LITTLE_ENDIAN) {
				value = data[0] +
				    (data[1] << 8) + (data[2] << 16)
				    + ((uint32_t)data[3] << 24);
			} else {
				value = data[3] +
				    (data[2] << 8) + (data[1] << 16)
				    + ((uint32_t)data[0] << 24);
			}
		}

		new_values[i] = value;

		if (!p_bit) {
			if (u_bit)
				addr += sizeof(uint32_t);
			else
				addr -= sizeof(uint32_t);
		}
	}

	for (i=(u_bit? 0 : 15); i>=0 && i<=15; i+=(u_bit? 1 : -1)) {
		if (!((iw >> i) & 1)) {
			/*  Skip register i:  */
			continue;
		}

		if (!s_bit) {
			cpu->cd.arm.r[i] = new_values[i];
		} else {
			switch (cpu->cd.arm.cpsr & ARM_FLAG_MODE) {
			case ARM_MODE_USR32:
			case ARM_MODE_SYS32:
				cpu->cd.arm.r[i] = new_values[i];
				break;
			case ARM_MODE_FIQ32:
				if (i >= 8 && i <= 14)
					cpu->cd.arm.default_r8_r14[i-8] =
					    new_values[i];
				else
					cpu->cd.arm.r[i] = new_values[i];
				break;
			case ARM_MODE_SVC32:
			case ARM_MODE_ABT32:
			case ARM_MODE_UND32:
			case ARM_MODE_IRQ32:
				if (i >= 13 && i <= 14)
					cpu->cd.arm.default_r8_r14[i-8] =
					    new_values[i];
				else
					cpu->cd.arm.r[i] = new_values[i];
				break;
			}
		}
	}

	if (w_bit)
		*np = addr;

	if (return_flag) {
		uint32_t new_cpsr;
		int switch_register_banks;

		switch (cpu->cd.arm.cpsr & ARM_FLAG_MODE) {
		case ARM_MODE_FIQ32:
			new_cpsr = cpu->cd.arm.spsr_fiq; break;
		case ARM_MODE_ABT32:
			new_cpsr = cpu->cd.arm.spsr_abt; break;
		case ARM_MODE_UND32:
			new_cpsr = cpu->cd.arm.spsr_und; break;
		case ARM_MODE_IRQ32:
			new_cpsr = cpu->cd.arm.spsr_irq; break;
		case ARM_MODE_SVC32:
			new_cpsr = cpu->cd.arm.spsr_svc; break;
		default:fatal("bdt_load: unimplemented mode %i\n",
			    cpu->cd.arm.cpsr & ARM_FLAG_MODE);
			cpu->running = 0;
			cpu->cd.arm.next_ic = &nothing_call;
			return;
		}

		switch_register_banks = (cpu->cd.arm.cpsr & ARM_FLAG_MODE) !=
		    (new_cpsr & ARM_FLAG_MODE);

		if (switch_register_banks)
			arm_save_register_bank(cpu);

		cpu->cd.arm.cpsr = new_cpsr;
		cpu->cd.arm.flags = cpu->cd.arm.cpsr >> 28;

		if (switch_register_banks)
			arm_load_register_bank(cpu);
	}

	/*  NOTE: Special case: Loading the PC  */
	if (iw & 0x8000) {
		cpu->pc = cpu->cd.arm.r[ARM_PC] & 0xfffffffc;
		if (cpu->machine->show_trace_tree)
			cpu_functioncall_trace_return(cpu);
		/*  TODO: There is no need to update the
		    pointers if this is a return to the
		    same page!  */
		/*  Find the new physical page and update the
		    translation pointers:  */
		quick_pc_to_pointers_arm(cpu);
	}
}


/*
 *  Shared between regular ARM and the THUMB encoded 'push'.
 */
void arm_push(struct cpu* cpu, uint32_t* np, int p_bit, int u_bit, int s_bit, int w_bit, uint16_t regs)
{
	int i;
	uint32_t value, addr = *np;
	unsigned char data[4];
	unsigned char *page;

	for (i=(u_bit? 0 : 15); i>=0 && i<=15; i+=(u_bit? 1 : -1)) {
		if (!((regs >> i) & 1)) {
			/*  Skip register i:  */
			continue;
		}

		value = cpu->cd.arm.r[i];

		if (s_bit) {
			switch (cpu->cd.arm.cpsr & ARM_FLAG_MODE) {
			case ARM_MODE_FIQ32:
				if (i >= 8 && i <= 14)
					value = cpu->cd.arm.default_r8_r14[i-8];
				break;
			case ARM_MODE_ABT32:
			case ARM_MODE_UND32:
			case ARM_MODE_IRQ32:
			case ARM_MODE_SVC32:
				if (i >= 13 && i <= 14)
					value = cpu->cd.arm.default_r8_r14[i-8];
				break;
			case ARM_MODE_USR32:
			case ARM_MODE_SYS32:
				break;
			}
		}

		/*  NOTE/TODO: 8 vs 12 on some ARMs  */
		if (i == ARM_PC)
			value = cpu->pc + 12;

		if (p_bit) {
			if (u_bit)
				addr += sizeof(uint32_t);
			else
				addr -= sizeof(uint32_t);
		}

		page = cpu->cd.arm.host_store[addr >> 12];
		if (page != NULL) {
			uint32_t *p32 = (uint32_t *) page;
			/*  Change byte order of value if
			    host and emulated endianness differ:  */
#ifdef HOST_LITTLE_ENDIAN
			if (cpu->byte_order == EMUL_BIG_ENDIAN)
#else
			if (cpu->byte_order == EMUL_LITTLE_ENDIAN)
#endif
				value = ((value & 0xff) << 24) |
				    ((value & 0xff00) << 8) |
				    ((value & 0xff0000) >> 8) |
				    ((value & 0xff000000) >> 24);
			p32[(addr & 0xfff) >> 2] = value;
		} else {
			if (cpu->byte_order == EMUL_LITTLE_ENDIAN) {
				data[0] = value;
				data[1] = value >> 8;
				data[2] = value >> 16;
				data[3] = value >> 24;
			} else {
				data[0] = value >> 24;
				data[1] = value >> 16;
				data[2] = value >> 8;
				data[3] = value;
			}
			/*  #524 (armbdt): the STM side of the same defect; see
			    the LDM site in arm_pop() for the full reasoning.
			    STM(1) p.A4-190 carries the identical
			    ignore-the-low-two-bits rule, and `*np = addr' below
			    must keep seeing the UNMASKED running value.  */
			if (!cpu->memory_rw(cpu, cpu->mem, addr & 0xfffffffc, data,
			    sizeof(data), MEM_WRITE, CACHE_DATA)) {
				/*  store failed  */
				return;
			}
		}

		if (!p_bit) {
			if (u_bit)
				addr += sizeof(uint32_t);
			else
				addr -= sizeof(uint32_t);
		}
	}

	if (w_bit)
		*np = addr;
}


/*
 *  bdt_load:  Block Data Transfer, Load
 *
 *  arg[0] = pointer to uint32_t in host memory, pointing to the base register
 *  arg[1] = 32-bit instruction word. Most bits are read from this.
 */
X(bdt_load)
{
	uint32_t *np = (uint32_t *)ic->arg[0];
	uint32_t low_pc;
	uint32_t iw = ic->arg[1];  /*  xxxx100P USWLnnnn llllllll llllllll  */
	int p_bit = iw & 0x01000000;
	int u_bit = iw & 0x00800000;
	int s_bit = iw & 0x00400000;
	int w_bit = iw & 0x00200000;

#ifdef GATHER_BDT_STATISTICS
	if (!s_bit)
		update_bdt_statistics(iw);
#endif

	/*  Synchronize the program counter:  */
	low_pc = ((size_t)ic - (size_t)
	    cpu->cd.arm.cur_ic_page) / sizeof(struct arm_instr_call);
	cpu->pc &= ~((ARM_IC_ENTRIES_PER_PAGE-1) << ARM_INSTR_ALIGNMENT_SHIFT);
	cpu->pc += (low_pc << ARM_INSTR_ALIGNMENT_SHIFT);

	arm_pop(cpu, np, p_bit, u_bit, s_bit, w_bit, (uint16_t)iw);
}
Y(bdt_load)


/*
 *  bdt_store:  Block Data Transfer, Store
 *
 *  arg[0] = pointer to uint32_t in host memory, pointing to the base register
 *  arg[1] = 32-bit instruction word. Most bits are read from this.
 */
X(bdt_store)
{
	uint32_t *np = (uint32_t *)ic->arg[0];
	uint32_t low_pc;
	uint32_t iw = ic->arg[1];  /*  xxxx100P USWLnnnn llllllll llllllll  */
	int p_bit = iw & 0x01000000;
	int u_bit = iw & 0x00800000;
	int s_bit = iw & 0x00400000;
	int w_bit = iw & 0x00200000;

#ifdef GATHER_BDT_STATISTICS
	if (!s_bit)
		update_bdt_statistics(iw);
#endif

	/*  Synchronize the program counter:  */
	low_pc = ((size_t)ic - (size_t)
	    cpu->cd.arm.cur_ic_page) / sizeof(struct arm_instr_call);
	cpu->pc &= ~((ARM_IC_ENTRIES_PER_PAGE-1) << ARM_INSTR_ALIGNMENT_SHIFT);
	cpu->pc += (low_pc << ARM_INSTR_ALIGNMENT_SHIFT);

	arm_push(cpu, np, p_bit, u_bit, s_bit, w_bit, (uint16_t)iw);
}
Y(bdt_store)


/*
 *  Load Register Exclusive (ARM "load linked"):
 *
 *  A Load Register Exclusive instruction initiates a RMW (read-modify-write)
 *  sequence.
 *
X(multi_0x08ac000c__ge);
X(multi_0x08a05018);


/*****************************************************************************/


/*
 *  netbsd_memset:
 *
 *  The core of a NetBSD/arm memset.
 *
 *  f01bc420:  e25XX080     subs    rX,rX,#0x80
 *  f01bc424:  a8ac000c     stmgeia ip!,{r2,r3}   (16 of these)
 *  ..
 *  f01bc464:  caffffed     bgt     0xf01bc420      <memset+0x38>
 */
X(netbsd_memset)
{
	unsigned char *page;
	uint32_t addr;

	do {
		addr = cpu->cd.arm.r[ARM_IP];

		instr(subs)(cpu, ic);

		if (((cpu->cd.arm.flags & ARM_F_N)?1:0) !=
		    ((cpu->cd.arm.flags & ARM_F_V)?1:0)) {
			cpu->n_translated_instrs += 16;
			/*  Skip the store multiples:  */
			cpu->cd.arm.next_ic = &ic[17];
			return;
		}

		/*  Crossing a page boundary? Then continue non-combined.  */
		if ((addr & 0xfff) + 128 > 0x1000)
			return;

		/*  R2/R3 non-zero? Not allowed here.  */
		if (cpu->cd.arm.r[2] != 0 || cpu->cd.arm.r[3] != 0)
			return;

		/*  printf("addr = 0x%08x\n", addr);  */

		page = cpu->cd.arm.host_store[addr >> 12];
		/*  No page translation? Continue non-combined.  */
		if (page == NULL)
			return;

		/*  Clear:  */
		memset(page + (addr & 0xfff), 0, 128);
		cpu->cd.arm.r[ARM_IP] = addr + 128;
		cpu->n_translated_instrs += 16;

		/*  Branch back if greater:  */
		cpu->n_translated_instrs += 1;
	} while (((cpu->cd.arm.flags & ARM_F_N)?1:0) ==
	    ((cpu->cd.arm.flags & ARM_F_V)?1:0) &&
	    !(cpu->cd.arm.flags & ARM_F_Z));

	/*  Continue at the instruction after the bgt:  */
	cpu->cd.arm.next_ic = &ic[18];
}


/*
 *  netbsd_memcpy:
 *
 *  The core of a NetBSD/arm memcpy.
 *
 *  f01bc530:  e8b15018     ldmia   r1!,{r3,r4,ip,lr}
 *  f01bc534:  e8a05018     stmia   r0!,{r3,r4,ip,lr}
 *  f01bc538:  e8b15018     ldmia   r1!,{r3,r4,ip,lr}
 *  f01bc53c:  e8a05018     stmia   r0!,{r3,r4,ip,lr}
 *  f01bc540:  e2522020     subs    r2,r2,#0x20
 *  f01bc544:  aafffff9     bge     0xf01bc530
 */
X(netbsd_memcpy)
{
	unsigned char *page_0, *page_1;
	uint32_t addr_r0, addr_r1;
	/*
	 *  #358: count the 32-byte iterations this call actually folded, so the
	 *  marker can be COUNT-AND-SUMMARISE. Unlike the other four marked folds
	 *  this handler has a real loop, so one DISPATCH emits one line however
	 *  many iterations it folded. A copy that never straddles a page is a
	 *  single dispatch and therefore a single line; a straddling copy bails,
	 *  runs one genuine iteration, and the back-branch re-dispatches this
	 *  slot, so it costs roughly one line per crossing. (An earlier draft of
	 *  this comment said "one line per call by construction", which is false
	 *  for exactly the straddling case the next paragraph describes -- a 1 MB
	 *  copy bails about 512 times. The flood conclusion survives: lines track
	 *  crossings, not the 32-byte iterations, and stay far below the
	 *  per-dispatch volume accepted for the loopless folds.)
	 *
	 *  The guard on the mid-loop markers below is about TRUTHFULNESS, not
	 *  flood control, and must not be confused with strlen's `n_loops > 1`
	 *  guard, which exists to suppress low-information lines. Both of this
	 *  fold's bail-outs are INSIDE the loop: a bail on the first iteration
	 *  folded nothing, while a bail on iteration k > 1 follows k-1 completed
	 *  32-byte copies. So a summary-only marker would under-report every
	 *  page-straddling copy (a 1 MB copy bails about 512 times), and an
	 *  unguarded bail marker would over-report the zero-work entries at page
	 *  ends. Reporting the count is what makes a row able to check that the
	 *  pieces sum to the copy the register advance implies.
	 */
	unsigned n_iter = 0;

	do {
		addr_r0 = cpu->cd.arm.r[0];
		addr_r1 = cpu->cd.arm.r[1];

		/*  printf("addr_r0 = %08x  r1 = %08x\n", addr_r0, addr_r1);  */

		/*  Crossing a page boundary? Then continue non-combined.  */
		if ((addr_r0 & 0xfff) + 32 > 0x1000 ||
		    (addr_r1 & 0xfff) + 32 > 0x1000) {
			if (n_iter)			/*  #358  */
				debugmsg_cpu(cpu, SUBSYS_CPU, "netbsd_memcpy",
				    VERBOSITY_DEBUG,
				    "combined %u iterations, page boundary",
				    n_iter);
			instr(multi_0x08b15018)(cpu, ic);
			return;
		}

		page_0 = cpu->cd.arm.host_store[addr_r0 >> 12];
		page_1 = cpu->cd.arm.host_store[addr_r1 >> 12];

		/*  No page translations? Continue non-combined.  */
		if (page_0 == NULL || page_1 == NULL) {
			if (n_iter)			/*  #358  */
				debugmsg_cpu(cpu, SUBSYS_CPU, "netbsd_memcpy",
				    VERBOSITY_DEBUG,
				    "combined %u iterations, no page", n_iter);
			instr(multi_0x08b15018)(cpu, ic);
			return;
		}

		/*  #354: the real `ldmia r1!,{r3,r4,ip,lr}` leaves those four
		    registers holding the LAST 16 bytes loaded -- the final
		    iteration's SECOND ldmia (src+16..+31). The fold moved the
		    bytes with memcpy but never wrote the registers, so the guest
		    read them stale ("publishes only r0/r1"). Direct host read,
		    NO byteswap, base masked to a word -- matching the handler
		    this fold emulates (multi_0x08b15018, generated by
		    generate_arm_multi.c:117: `addr &= 0xffc`, then a plain
		    `r[k] = p[k]`) and the page-cross / NULL-page bail-outs above,
		    which delegate to that same handler. So fold == handler ==
		    bail-out: an on-page copy cannot leave different registers
		    than an identical copy that straddles a page. Each iteration
		    overwrites r3/r4/ip/lr, so the final iteration's block
		    persists, as the architecture leaves it.

		    The mask is NOT cosmetic. ARM's LDM ignores addr[1:0] rather
		    than requiring alignment, and this matcher matches code SHAPE,
		    not register values, so a guest with r1 & 3 != 0 does fold:
		    measured at r1 = SRC+1, the masked read publishes what the
		    genuine sequence publishes, while `& 0xfff` would publish
		    byte-rotated words. `(r1 & 0xfff) + 16 <= 0xff0` rules out a
		    carry past bit 11, so (r1 & 0xffc) + 16 is bit-for-bit the
		    address the real second ldmia reads. Bound: the entry guard
		    gives (r1 & 0xfff) <= 0xfe0, and masking only lowers that, so
		    +16..+31 stays inside the page.

		    Published BEFORE the memcpy, and NOT because that makes
		    overlap safe -- it does not. The real second ldmia runs AFTER
		    the first stmia, so on a FORWARD overlap it loads post-store
		    bytes, which no placement around a single memcpy can
		    reproduce (measured: dst = src+16 diverges either way). The
		    reason is determinism: publish-after would read bytes a
		    UB-on-overlap memcpy had just written, so the registers would
		    depend on the host memcpy's direction and vector width.
		    Publish-before is identical to publish-after in every case
		    this fold is correct in (no overlap) and deterministic
		    otherwise. The overlap and unaligned-copy divergences are
		    pre-existing and recorded in OUTSTANDING_BUGS.

		    #378: "NO byteswap" above is safe ONLY because this matcher
		    can no longer fire for a big-endian guest: it requires
		    instr(multi_0x08b15018) installed at ic[-5], and the
		    dispatcher now installs the multi fast path only when
		    cpu->byte_order == EMUL_LITTLE_ENDIAN (a BE guest gets
		    bdt_load there, the matcher declines, and this raw pw[] block
		    is unreachable). If a future round re-enables the fast path
		    for BE (the S4 _le/_be shape), these four publishes must gain
		    the same swap in the SAME change or fold and handler diverge
		    -- exactly the class the #354 invariant above forbids.  */
		{
			uint32_t *pw = (uint32_t *)
			    (page_1 + (addr_r1 & 0xffc) + 16);
			cpu->cd.arm.r[3]      = pw[0];   /* +16 */
			cpu->cd.arm.r[4]      = pw[1];   /* +20 */
			cpu->cd.arm.r[ARM_IP] = pw[2];   /* +24  (r12) */
			cpu->cd.arm.r[ARM_LR] = pw[3];   /* +28  (r14) */
		}

		memcpy(page_0 + (addr_r0 & 0xfff),
		    page_1 + (addr_r1 & 0xfff), 32);
		cpu->cd.arm.r[0] = addr_r0 + 32;
		cpu->cd.arm.r[1] = addr_r1 + 32;
		n_iter ++;				/*  #358  */

		cpu->n_translated_instrs += 4;

		instr(subs)(cpu, ic + 4);
		cpu->n_translated_instrs ++;

		/*  Loop while greater or equal:  */
		cpu->n_translated_instrs ++;
	} while (((cpu->cd.arm.flags & ARM_F_N)?1:0) ==
	    ((cpu->cd.arm.flags & ARM_F_V)?1:0));

	/*  #358: normal exit -- summarise. Unguarded, because reaching here means
	    the loop body ran at least once (the bail-outs return early), so the
	    count is always >= 1.  */
	debugmsg_cpu(cpu, SUBSYS_CPU, "netbsd_memcpy",
	    VERBOSITY_DEBUG, "combined %u iterations", n_iter);

	/*  Continue at the instruction after the bge:  */
	cpu->cd.arm.next_ic = &ic[6];
	cpu->n_translated_instrs --;
}


	}

	d->int_assert = 1;
}


/*
 *  wdc__write():
 */
void wdc__write(struct cpu *cpu, struct wdc_data *d)
{
	int cyl = d->cyl_hi * 256+ d->cyl_lo;
	int count = d->seccnt? d->seccnt : 256;
	uint64_t offset = 512 * (d->sector - 1
	    + (int64_t)d->head * d->sectors_per_track[d->drive] +
	    (int64_t)d->heads[d->drive] * d->sectors_per_track[d->drive] * cyl);
#if 0
	/*  LBA:  */
	if (d->lba)
		offset = 512 * (((d->head & 0xf) << 24) +
		    (cyl << 8) + d->sector);
	printf("WDC write to offset %lli\n", (long long)offset);
#endif

	d->write_in_progress = d->cur_command;
	d->write_count = count;
	d->write_offset = offset;

	/*  TODO: result code?  */
}


/*
 *  status_byte():
 *
 *  Return a reasonable status byte corresponding to the controller's current
 *  state.
 */
static int status_byte(struct wdc_data *d, struct cpu *cpu)
{
	int odata = 0;
	if (diskimage_exist(cpu->machine, d->drive + d->base_drive,
	    DISKIMAGE_IDE))
		odata |= WDCS_DRDY | WDCS_DSC;
	if (d->inbuf_head != d->inbuf_tail)
		odata |= WDCS_DRQ;
	if (d->write_in_progress)
		odata |= WDCS_DRQ;
	if (d->error)
		odata |= WDCS_ERR;
	if (d->atapi_cmd_in_progress && (d->atapi_phase & WDCS_DRQ)) {
		odata |= WDCS_DRQ;
	}
	return odata;
}


/*  #481: forward-declared so the ALT-CTRL arm below can call it.  A panel seat's
    control mutant failed to compile for want of exactly this line and the failure
    was briefly read as a design constraint against calling the shared helper; it
    is one line and it builds clean.  */
static void wdc_new_command_reset(struct wdc_data *d);

DEVICE_ACCESS(wdc_altstatus)
{
	struct wdc_data *d = (struct wdc_data *) extra;
	uint64_t idata = 0, odata = 0;

	idata = data[0];

	/*  Same as the normal status byte:  */
	odata = status_byte(d, cpu);

	if (writeflag==MEM_READ) {
		if (relative_addr != 0) {
			/*  #525 (wdcaltdigin): THE READ PATH NEVER DECODED ITS
			    OFFSET EITHER.  `relative_addr' was tested in exactly
			    one place in this function -- inside the `else' of
			    this `if' -- so a READ of offset 1, the Drive Address
			    register (0x3f7 on the PC layout), returned the
			    ALT-STATUS byte -- the value of a DIFFERENT register,
			    from an offset this handler never decoded.

			    THIS IS THE SYMMETRIC CELL OF #494, WHICH CLOSED THE
			    WRITE HALF OF THE SAME `if' AND LEFT THIS ONE OPEN.
			    The function's own inputs give a two-by-two --
			    {READ, WRITE} x {offset 0, offset 1} -- and three
			    cells were closed.  Measured at rung 3 on the
			    committed build: 14/16 pre-fix with both offset-1
			    read rows returning the status byte (0x51 at power-on,
			    0x58 armed), 16/16 after.

			    ZERO IS AN EMULATOR POLICY, NOT A HARDWARE CLAIM.
			    DC21285-era PC parts leave 0x3f7 to the floppy
			    controller -- wdcreg.h compiles wd_digin out under an
			    `#if 0' reading "NOT MAPPED; fd uses this register on
			    PCs", and dev_fdc.c's DEV_FDC_LENGTH carries "TODO 8,
			    but collision with wdc".  Whose register it really is
			    stays open (row wdcaltownership).  What is settled is
			    that it is not ours to answer with our status byte, so
			    the declined read returns the deterministic 0 that #95
			    already pre-zeroes the caller's buffer with, rather
			    than inventing a value.

			    SILENT, like #494's write decline and for a NARROWER
			    version of the same reason.  What the tree actually
			    establishes is that on the PC layout this byte is
			    CONTESTED and documented from both sides; it does NOT
			    establish that every guest on every registered layout
			    expects a floppy effect here, and an earlier draft of
			    this comment claimed that.  A diagnostic would
			    therefore be announcing a decode we are not certain is
			    anomalous -- which is reason enough to stay quiet,
			    without the stronger claim.

			    (NOTE the collision is PROSPECTIVE, not live:
			    DEV_FDC_LENGTH is 6, so dev_fdc.c does not register
			    0x3f7 today.  The "TODO 8" is what would collide.)

			    THE REGISTRATION IS DELIBERATELY NOT NARROWED.  A
			    2 -> 1 at the memory_device_register() below would
			    close both offset-1 cells structurally, but it moves
			    0x3f7 from HANDLED-AND-DECLINED to UNMAPPED -- a
			    different path whose behaviour here is unmeasured --
			    and it would leave #494's arm as dead code carrying a
			    measured comment.  Owner ruling: decode the read.  */
			odata = 0;
		} else
			debug("[ wdc: read from ALTSTATUS: 0x%02x ]\n",
			    (int)odata);
	} else if (relative_addr != 0) {
		/*  #494: THE HANDLER NEVER DECODED ITS OWN OFFSET.  The region is
		    registered TWO BYTES wide, so offset 1 -- the Drive Address
		    register, 0x3f7 on the PC layout -- arrived here and was treated
		    as a Device Control write.  Since #481 corrected the reset bit,
		    that meant a guest store to a READ-ONLY register aborted a live
		    ATA write and purged the queued ring.  Measured at rung 3 on an
		    unmodified evbmips -e malta; the witness is preserved under
		    regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/witness_20260829/.

		    THE WHOLE ACTION IS DECLINED, NOT JUST THE HELPER CALL, AND THAT
		    IS THE POINT.  The reset has two effects with two owners: the
		    cur_command assignment below, and the ring/DRQ clearing inside
		    wdc_new_command_reset().  A fix that guards only the helper leaves
		    the ATAPI signature latched.  That was MEASURED, not reasoned: it
		    escapes the witness AND the detector that replaced it with a clean
		    sweep, because every row in both observes DRQ or the ring and
		    neither varies under it.  The kill table and its scores are under
		    regress/artifacts/wdcaltoffset/killtable_20260830/ -- read TABLE.txt
		    for the counts rather than trusting a number written here, which is
		    how this sentence was wrong once already.

		    SILENT BY DESIGN, at debug() level and with no latch.  The house
		    idiom announces a guest ANOMALY, and this is not one: the tree
		    records in two places that this byte belongs to the floppy
		    controller on PCs -- wdcreg.h compiles wd_digin out under
		    an `#if 0` whose own note reads "NOT MAPPED; fd uses this
		    register on PCs" (the delimiters are dropped here on purpose:
		    a comment terminator quoted inside a comment ends it), and
		    dev_fdc.c's DEV_FDC_LENGTH carries `TODO 8, but collision with
		    wdc`.  A correct guest writes here expecting a floppy effect, so
		    a wdc diagnostic would report correct behaviour as a fault.

		    THE ACCESS STAYS HANDLED (return 1), AND THE REASON IS NOT THE ONE
		    THIS COMMENT FIRST GAVE.  It said a 0 would fall through to
		    unassigned-address handling and warn per access.  That is false: this
		    device is REGISTERED, so a 0 never reaches that path.  memory_rw sets
		    res = -1, takes its res <= 0 branch, and raises EXCEPTION_DBE --
		    measured, 19793 `device 'wdc_altstatus' ... failed` lines and ZERO
		    `non-existant` ones under -v.  The guest then loops forever
		    re-executing the faulting store, and at DEFAULT verbosity it does so
		    in SILENCE.  That is worse than a warning flood, not better, which is
		    why the wrong reason was worth correcting rather than dropping: a
		    maintainer who reads `only noisy warnings` may decide a 0 is fine.

		    The arm falls through to the common tail, which is a no-op for it --
		    the tail only assigns on the READ path.  (An earlier draft justified
		    that by the untouched-buffer hazard; #95 already zeroes the caller's
		    buffer, so that hazard cannot arise here either.)  */
		debug("[ wdc: write to DRIVE ADDRESS (read-only): 0x%02x,"
		    " ignored ]\n", (int)idata);
	} else {
		debug("[ wdc: write to ALT. CTRL: 0x%02x ]\n",
		    (int)idata);
		/*  #481: THE RESET BIT IS WDCTL_RST (0x04), NOT WDCTL_4BIT (0x08).
		
		    WDCTL_4BIT is "use four head bits (wd1003)", a head-addressing
		    mode selector that Linux libata holds as its STEADY-STATE ap->ctl.
		    Testing it here meant three measured defects at once:
		
		      * a real soft reset was MISSED.  Older OpenBSD asserts with 0x06,
		        which has bit 3 CLEAR -- measured `cur_command 0x0`.
		      * a ROUTINE control write DESTROYED THE RECORD OF WHICH COMMAND IS
		        EXECUTING -- measured `0xec -> 0x100`.  cur_command is also the
		        value assigned into write_in_progress, so this is only harmless
		        because wdc__write() has exactly ONE caller, immediately after
		        the assignment.  An accident, not an invariant.
		      * an nIEN write latched a reset.  0x0a is libata's polled-mode
		        staple -- measured `0x0a->0x100`.
		
		    COMMAND_RESET IS NOT A RESET ACTION.  It is read at exactly two
		    sites, both making the cylinder registers return the ATAPI signature
		    0xEB14 for a CD-ROM.  It is the SIGNATURE LATCH -- which is why the
		    wrong bit went unnoticed: triggering on libata's steady state kept
		    the signature on almost always.
		
		    LEVEL-TRIGGERED, DELIBERATELY, AND THE MEASURED DIFFERENCE
		    FAVOURS THIS FORM.  A channel held in reset must not carry bytes
		    out the far side, and an edge-triggered abort lets it:
		
		        assert 0x0e, 8 guest stores to DATA, rewrite 0x0e, release
		          LEVEL (this form):  occ 0, DRQ 0   -- the ring is purged
		          EDGE:               occ 8, DRQ 1   -- the bytes survive the
		                                                 whole reset, and the
		                                                 status byte still
		                                                 advertises DRQ
		
		    Measured through register writes only, with a liveness control (the
		    same traffic without a reset leaves occ 8, so the 0 is the reset
		    working and not a dead path).
		
		    *** AN EARLIER VERSION OF THIS COMMENT SAID edge and level were
		    "indistinguishable here, because the abort is idempotent and no
		    protocol-following driver issues commands while holding SRST".
		    THAT WAS MEASURED FALSE.  Three things were wrong with it:
		
		      * the differential behind it only ever asserted SRST ONCE, and
		        the forms differ only when a driver HOLDS it across two
		        control writes with something accruing in between;
		      * IDEMPOTENCE OF THE HELPER DOES NOT LICENCE THE INFERENCE.
		        It means repeating the abort is harmless.  It says nothing
		        about SKIPPING the repeat, which is what an edge does, and
		        state accrues between the writes;
		      * the scenario above ISSUES NO COMMAND.  Plain data-register
		        stores suffice -- and in a hardening fork the guest need not
		        be protocol-following at all.
			debug("[ wdc: read from CYL_HI: 0x%02x ]\n",(int)odata);
		} else {
			d->cyl_hi = idata;
			debug("[ wdc: write to CYL_HI: 0x%02x ]\n", (int)idata);
		}
		break;

	case wd_sdh:	/*  6: sectorsize/drive/head  */
		if (writeflag==MEM_READ) {
			odata = (d->sectorsize << 6) + (d->lba << 5) +
			    (d->drive << 4) + (d->head);
			debug("[ wdc: read from SDH: 0x%02x (sectorsize %i,"
			    " lba=%i, drive %i, head %i) ]\n", (int)odata,
			    d->sectorsize, d->lba, d->drive, d->head);
		} else {
			d->sectorsize = (idata >> 6) & 3;
			d->lba   = (idata >> 5) & 1;
			d->drive = (idata >> 4) & 1;
			d->head  = idata & 0xf;
			debug("[ wdc: write to SDH: 0x%02x (sectorsize %i,"
			    " lba=%i, drive %i, head %i) ]\n", (int)idata,
			d->int_assert = 0;		} else {
			debug("[ wdc: write to COMMAND: 0x%02x ]\n",(int)idata);
			wdc_command(cpu, d, idata);
		}
		break;

	default:
		if (writeflag==MEM_READ)
			debug("[ wdc: read from 0x%02x ]\n",
			    (int)relative_addr);
		else
			debug("[ wdc: write to  0x%02x: 0x%02x ]\n",
			    (int)relative_addr, (int)idata);
	}

	/*  Assert interrupt, if necessary:  */
	dev_wdc_tick(cpu, extra);

		else {
			/*  #464: latch the announcement only.  This sits
			    INSIDE the function's common `ret:` tail, so
			    `data[0] = odata` and the `return 1` below must run
			    regardless -- an early return here would hand the
			    guest an untouched read buffer.  */
			if (len != 1 && !d->warned_nonbyte_read) {
				d->warned_nonbyte_read = 1;
				fatal("[ wdc: WARNING! non-8-bit access on "
				    "READ! ]\n");
			}
			data[0] = odata;
		}
	}

	return 1;
}


DEVINIT(wdc)
{
	struct wdc_data *d;
	uint64_t alt_status_addr;
	int i, tick_shift = WDC_TICK_SHIFT;

	CHECK_ALLOCATION(d = (struct wdc_data *) malloc(sizeof(struct wdc_data)));
	memset(d, 0, sizeof(struct wdc_data));

	INTERRUPT_CONNECT(devinit->interrupt_path, d->irq);
	d->addr_mult  = devinit->addr_mult;
	d->data_debug = 1;
	d->io_enabled = 1;
	d->error      = 1;

	d->inbuf = (unsigned char *) zeroed_alloc(WDC_INBUF_SIZE);

	/*  base_drive = 0 for the primary controller, 2 for the secondary.  */
	d->base_drive = 0;
	if ((devinit->addr & 0xfff) == 0x170)
		d->base_drive = 2;

	alt_status_addr = devinit->addr + 0x206;

	/*  Special hacks for individual machines:  */
	switch (devinit->machine->machine_type) {
	case MACHINE_MACPPC:
		alt_status_addr = devinit->addr + 0x160;
		break;
	case MACHINE_HPCMIPS:
		/*  TODO: Fix  */
		if (devinit->addr == 0x14000180)
			alt_status_addr = 0x14000386;
		break;
	case MACHINE_IQ80321:
		alt_status_addr = devinit->addr + 0x402;
		break;
	}

	/*  Get disk geometries:  */
	for (i=0; i<2; i++)
		if (diskimage_exist(devinit->machine, d->base_drive +i,
		    DISKIMAGE_IDE))
			diskimage_getchs(devinit->machine, d->base_drive + i,
			    DISKIMAGE_IDE, &d->cyls[i], &d->heads[i],
			    &d->sectors_per_track[i]);

	memory_device_register(devinit->machine->memory, "wdc_altstatus",
	    alt_status_addr, 2, dev_wdc_altstatus_access, d, DM_DEFAULT, NULL);
	memory_device_register(devinit->machine->memory, devinit->name,
	    devinit->addr, DEV_WDC_LENGTH * devinit->addr_mult, dev_wdc_access,
	    d, DM_DEFAULT, NULL);

	machine_add_tickfunction(devinit->machine, dev_wdc_tick,
	    d, tick_shift);

	devinit->return_ptr = d;

	return 1;
}
